    Source documentation

    Sorting and collecting is a sound reflection procedure in any abelian group.

    theorem eval_normalize (f : α → A) (le : α → α → Bool) (ps : Poly α) :
    eval f (normalize le ps) = eval f ps := by
…

  13. theorem OAI.NuclearUltrapower.SOS.support_normalize line 78

    Source documentation

    Collection never introduces a new group element.

    theorem support_normalize (le : α → α → Bool) (ps : Poly α) {g : α} :
    g ∈ (normalize le ps).map Prod.fst → g ∈ ps.map Prod.fst := by
…
